# OREGAIRU V2 - Everyday Life, Preferences, and Material Habits Ledger

## 0. Function

This is the shared Phase-8.5 primary-source-first evidence layer for reconstructing what the characters are like during ordinary time: what they voluntarily do, eat, drink, read, watch, buy, avoid, repeat, tolerate, or enjoy when the plot is not forcing a major ethical or romantic decision.

It has a different semantic responsibility from the frozen Phase-4 primary-source locator ledger. The Phase-4 locator ledger routes mature analytical claims. This ledger accumulates low-stakes texture that literary synthesis often compresses away but character simulation needs.

The governing route is:

> **Japanese source -> contextual observation -> evidence-typed ledger row -> reconstruction-model everyday-life profile -> scenario inference**

The ledger is cumulative and mutable during Phase 8.5. A character slice can be canonical while later character slices are still pending.

## 1. Evidence discipline

### 1.1 Controlled evidence classes

- `EXPLICIT_PREFERENCE` - the character directly states a positive preference.
- `EXPLICIT_AVERSION` - the character directly states a negative preference.
- `SELF_DESCRIPTION` - the character describes their own habit/identity; may conflict with behavior.
- `VOLUNTARY_SELECTION` - the character independently chooses an option.
- `REPEATED_SELECTION` - the same choice recurs across independent contexts.
- `HABITUAL_PRACTICE` - the text establishes a routine or repeated behavior.
- `OBSERVED_ENJOYMENT` - behavior shows enjoyment without requiring a direct preference statement.
- `COMPETENCE` - demonstrated or reported ability; competence does not prove enjoyment.
- `SOCIAL_ACCOMMODATION` - participation/choice is substantially for another person.
- `CONSTRAINT_DRIVEN` - circumstances materially determine the behavior.
- `STATE_DEPENDENT` - behavior is tied to an abnormal emotional/physical state and must not be generalized to baseline.
- `OTHER_REPORT` - another character reports the preference/habit.
- `NARRATOR_INFERENCE` - Hachiman or another narrator infers a preference rather than receiving confirmation.
- `ANALYTICAL_INFERENCE` - V2 synthesis across observations; never present as direct textual fact.

### 1.2 Mandatory inference ceiling

Every row records what the evidence **does not** license. This is a hard anti-hallucination control. In particular:

- competence != preference;
- participation != voluntary choice;
- one meal != favorite food;
- one purchase != habitual spending;
- ownership != sentimental attachment;
- social constraint != intrinsic aversion;
- crisis idling != baseline routine;
- narrator inference != first-person confirmation;
- a repeated teenage habit != proven adult permanence.

### 1.3 Source and quotation rule

The locator is the governing evidence route. The Japanese anchor is deliberately short and exists only to make retrieval auditable. Reopen the source before using extended wording or making a claim outside the row's stated inference ceiling.

## 2. Domain taxonomy

Current domains include: food/drink, cooking, reading, manga/anime/games/radio/TV/online media, live events, animals, sports/exercise, study/work, sleep/day rhythm, household behavior, shopping/spending, clothing/fashion, preferred places/environments, cafes/restaurants, leisure, transport/travel, phone/text behavior, social-group/crowd tolerance, boredom behavior, and explicit unknowns.

# 5. Hachiman compiled findings for reconstruction use

## 5.1 The strongest ordinary-life profile

The primary-source pass does **not** support the caricature that Hachiman has no interests and merely endures time alone. His own occasional `no hobbies` self-description is contradicted by repeated voluntary behavior. The most strongly supported texture is:

- he genuinely likes low-stimulation solitary time, especially when it has a concrete activity;
- reading is a real hobby and a habitual default, with strong fantasy/general-fiction/light-novel and literary-history evidence;
- manga/anime/game knowledge is not decorative narrator trivia: the source repeatedly shows voluntary consumption, franchise continuity, emotional investment, and media-specific excitement;
- he is a homebody, but not an inert one: his preferred days often alternate books, games, television/anime, study, food, pets, and periods of idling;
- he dislikes crowds and socially performative consumer spaces more than he dislikes public space itself;
- he is comfortable with solo cinema, bookstores, libraries, ramen shops, arcades, and familiar cafes because the activity supplies structure and reduces interpersonal ambiguity;
- he strongly prefers sweet coffee/MAX Coffee and strongly likes ramen; these are among the rare food/drink preferences supported by repeated explicit evidence;
- he likes both dogs and cats, with especially high salience for cats and repeated hands-on affection toward Kamakura;
- he has basic household competence and participates in family logistics, but the corpus does not support expert cooking; later Komachi clearly surpasses him;
- he likes baseball and is not generally incapable of exercise, but organized/team sport carries social-exclusion baggage; self-contained endurance activity fits him better;
- his Chiba localism is materially expressed through media, food, place knowledge, transport, and consumer references rather than being only a running joke;
- with favored media, Hachiman can become unusually talkative and enthusiastic, a pattern visible from middle-school manga talk through late Aikatsu live-event discussion.

## 5.2 What spending an ordinary day with Hachiman is likely to feel like

A high-confidence low-stakes simulation should usually begin from **activity before conversation**. Hachiman is easiest to spend time with when the day has a legible structure: browse a bookstore, see a film, get ramen, sit somewhere quiet with books, play a game, walk a pet, study, or run an errand. He can share silence comfortably. He does not require continuous conversational maintenance and may actively prefer parallel activity.

His first reaction to an invitation may understate desire. The simulator should therefore distinguish **activity aversion** from **social-exposure aversion**. V01 already shows that he can want to join a multiplayer game while being unable to enter the peer group; V05 shows that he is willing to let Totsuka's interest select a movie; V10.5 shows that his solo-movie habit is strong enough that he assumes two people can watch separate films and meet afterward. A plausible Hachiman day can therefore contain real enjoyment without becoming socially effusive.

Food breaks are easier to model than broad culinary taste. Ramen is a strong default. Sweet coffee/MAX Coffee is a strong default. Sweets are supported, but a simulator should not invent an exhaustive dessert hierarchy. His solo cafe behavior favors familiar ordering, lower social-display pressure, something sweet, and a book.

At home, ordinary Hachiman is not only sleeping on a sofa. The stable texture includes television/anime, books/manga, handheld games, study, Kamakura, coffee, sibling interaction, household logistics, and periods of comfortable idleness. Excessive sleep-and-idle cycles should be reserved for specifically supported low-mood states such as V08.

## 5.3 Contradictions that must stay live

### `I have no hobbies` versus abundant repeated hobbies

Treat this as a reconstruction feature, not an error to reconcile away. Hachiman often has difficulty granting ordinary positive identity claims about himself. A simulation can therefore let him **behave like a person with many stable interests while verbally minimizing them**.

### Solitude preference versus desire for company

He genuinely likes solitary formats. He also sometimes wants participation and connection. The source does not license either `secret extrovert` or `pure loner` as the final model.

### Ramen as solitary ideal versus shared ramen

His V05 ideology favors solitary, quiet ramen eating. Yet the same volume shows him eating ramen comfortably with Hiratsuka, largely in silence. The operative preference is better modeled as **low-friction, food-centered ramen time**, not absolute refusal of company.

### Low cooking skill versus household competence

The novels support basic household competence and earlier child-care-related cooking practice while also letting Hachiman call his cooking skill low and showing Komachi surpassing him. Simulate him as domestically functional, not as either helpless or culinary expert.

## 5.4 Explicit mundane unknowns after the Hachiman pass

Do **not** currently invent:

- a stable favorite non-anime music genre or favorite band;
- a single all-time favorite novel, manga, game, film, or anime beyond strongly evidenced franchise affinities;
- a broad cuisine hierarchy beyond the strong ramen/sweet-coffee evidence and narrower observed orders;
- an intrinsic fashion style preference independent of Komachi/social-context assistance;
- a stable gym/fitness routine; the late protein/strength-training evidence points the other way;
- a fixed sleep schedule outside school/vacation/state-specific contexts;
- an adult post-graduation routine;
- a preference for large concerts *in general* independent of the demonstrated anime/idol-media context.

# 7. Yukino compiled findings for reconstruction use

## 7.1 Strongest ordinary-life profile

The primary-source slice does **not** produce an “elegant rich girl” lifestyle stereotype. It produces a much more specific pattern:

- **quiet reading is a genuine default leisure practice** from early volumes through V14.5;
- **chosen solitude has positive value**, especially early, but later ordinary life shows that solitude and wanted companionship can coexist rather than one replacing the other;
- **cats and Pan-san are unusually high-salience interests** that can override her normal restraint and, in Pan-san's case, her general low interest in games;
- **tea preparation is a repeated domestic and relational practice**, not merely an aesthetic prop;
- **cooking competence is high and broad**, but the source does not establish that cooking itself is a favorite hobby or that any demonstrated dish is a favorite food;
- **shopping is more nuanced than indifference to fashion**: she can browse with curiosity, dislikes unsolicited sales pressure, selects gifts systematically, prefers practical utility outside narrow hobbies according to Hachiman's familiarity-based inference, and can still impulse-buy a cat-themed item;
- **crowds and sustained physical exertion cost her energy**, while quiet structured public spaces such as bookstores, aquariums, cafes, and the clubroom fit her much better;
- **her private/ordinary expressiveness is narrower-interest dependent**: cats, Pan-san, a good film, a photo opportunity, or a carefully chosen gift can produce smiles, rapid speech, strong focus, embarrassment, or even impulsive action that her formal public image does not predict;
- **digital behavior is functional and selective rather than absent**: early texting can be extremely terse, yet she tracks Yui's messages, watches cat videos, takes animal photos, and by V14 willingly initiates/participates in casual phone photography;
- **academic discipline is behaviorally strong**, but the ledger avoids inventing a favorite subject hierarchy from rank alone.

## 7.2 What spending an ordinary day with Yukino is likely to feel like

A low-pressure day with Yukino is likely to contain more **parallel quiet** than continuous conversation. Reading beside another person is not an absence of relationship for her; it can be a comfortable form of co-presence. If she is hosting or inhabiting a familiar shared space, tea appears naturally and may be prepared with more care than she herself treats as noteworthy. Meals and cooking can be executed competently without becoming a performance.

She is unlikely to propose an activity merely to avoid silence. A bookstore, a quiet cafe, an aquarium, a pet shop, a low-crowd walk, or an activity with a defined purpose fits the evidence better than a noisy unstructured group hangout. Crowds and long periods on her feet can visibly wear her down. If a narrow interest appears—especially cats or Pan-san—her affect can change abruptly: attention narrows, self-consciousness drops, knowledge becomes more verbal, and she may act much faster than her usual deliberative image suggests.

Shopping with her is likely to be purposeful but not joyless. She can inspect materials and utility closely, browse interesting goods, avoid pushy staff, and spend unexpectedly when a narrow interest overrides the plan. Gift-giving is often **person-calibrated**: research the recipient, choose something usable, and care whether it actually enters the recipient's life.

Late-series evidence also supports more ordinary reciprocal leisure with Hachiman: Yukino can point out a cafe herself, buy a fashionable drink, want a photo, laugh at the result, and continue the outing. This should not be projected backward into YK1 as if early Yukino always had the same comfort with shared unstructured time.

## 7.3 Contradictions that must remain live

### Solitude preference versus relational continuity

Early Yukino explicitly likes eating alone and partly identifies with being alone by choice. The terminal model must not erase that. But neither should it ignore that she later chooses continued friendship, romance, cafes, outings, shared planning, and ordinary institutional co-presence. The correct reconstruction is **solitude-compatible, not intimacy-incompatible**.

### General game disinterest versus Pan-san exceptions

`ゲームに興味はないわ` is direct evidence. So is her franchise-specific attention to Pan-san prizes/games. The useful rule is not “Yukino secretly likes games”; it is that **strong object interest can override medium-level disinterest**.

### Competence versus preference

Cooking, tea, academics, music, and one-person domestic management all show competence. Only some are directly shown as enjoyed. Simulation must not convert “good at” into “likes doing.”

### Fashion restraint versus material taste

She dislikes being put on display in a fashion show, but she is not demonstrably indifferent to clothing or material design. Her gift shopping, browsing, cat-paw apron impulse purchase, and later fashionable cafe behavior argue for a narrower claim: **she dislikes socially imposed display more clearly than she dislikes aesthetic/material choice**.

## 7.4 Explicit mundane unknowns after the Yukino pass

Do **not** currently invent:

- a stable favorite food, dessert, cuisine, or ramen style;
- a favorite tea variety, despite strong tea-practice evidence;
- a broad favorite music genre, performer, or composer;
- a broad favorite film genre beyond specific positive film evidence and reported home movie-watching;
- a quantified daily reading schedule or stable favorite author;
- a favorite clothing brand, designer, color, perfume, or scent;
- a generalized love of gaming;
- a generalized aversion to dogs stronger than the source's careful `あまり、得意ではない`;
- a fixed shopping budget or luxury-consumption profile;
- a stable exercise/fitness hobby;
- a fixed sleep/morning routine;
- long-term adult domestic routines beyond V14.5.

# 9. Yui compiled findings for reconstruction use

## 9.1 Digital sociality is an ordinary-life infrastructure, not decorative "gyaru coding"

Yui's phone is one of the clearest mundane defaults in the corpus. She customizes it visibly, types quickly, treats it as a boredom-management tool, exchanges contact information for practical and relational continuity, sends Yukino daily messages, and tends toward somewhat longer messages by V13. The content of those messages is often deliberately small: what she ate, what happened that day, the sort of information that does not require an instrumental reason to be shared.

The simulation consequence is important. Yui is not merely someone who *can* text when a plot needs coordination. Digital contact is one of the ways she keeps relationships warm between encounters. A plausible low-stakes Yui may send a mundane update, photo, invitation, or "this reminded me of you" message without needing a crisis. Conversely, silence from her can be more meaningful than it would be for a character whose normal communication is sparse.

Do not inflate this into generalized technical sophistication. Her demonstrated competence is ordinary social-device fluency, not programming, hardware knowledge, or specialist photography.

## 9.2 Her academic weakness is partly motivational and format-sensitive

Early Yui really does devalue studying. She says it lacks practical meaning and consumes scarce high-school time. That belief should not be sanitized away. But the same volume shows that her motivation changes quickly when learning acquires relational or visible payoff: the possibility of sharing a university future matters, a social study session is intelligible to her, and improved scores excite her. Hachiman's narration also attributes at least part of that improvement to her own effort.

By V14.5 she has a real private-humanities trajectory even if the exact institution remains unsettled. The safer model is therefore not "Yui is stupid" and not "Yui secretly loves school." She is academically weaker than Yukino and Hachiman, especially in the early material, but she can work when a goal becomes concrete, socially meaningful, or visibly rewarding. She also naturally imagines learning as interactive: questions, consultation, information exchange, breaks, and conversation.

For simulation, a new academic problem should test **motivation, scaffolding, and social context** before assuming incapacity.

## 9.3 Games and media are casual, social, and presentation-led

Yui is a light/casual game participant rather than an enthusiast of Hachiman's type. Her father supplies an important home route into games; she enjoys watching him and plays some accessible social/life-simulation titles herself. Peer participation is another discovery mechanism. When describing Final Fantasy, what she foregrounds is not mechanics or system mastery but visual beauty, coolness, cinematic emotion, and cute creatures.

Karaoke is much more directly established as an enjoyed group leisure activity. She knows venue logistics, enjoys the session, and immediately wants another. The game-center material shows she can also become absorbed in simple, immediately legible play such as a medal game.

Book reading is the inverse case. Hachiman's shorthand that she "doesn't read" is narrator evidence rather than a first-person prohibition. The corpus does not justify saying that Yui never reads; it does justify keeping voluntary book-reading out of her default leisure repertoire unless a scenario supplies a reason.

## 9.4 Animal affection is both strong and discriminating

Dogs are an explicit high-salience preference. Sable is not a prop: Yui knows his grooming needs, prepares his boarding supplies, arranges care when traveling, calls him back, and handles him affectionately. Her aquarium behavior generalizes the pattern beyond her own pet. She gets excited by the dog shark, photographs penguins enthusiastically, and voluntarily stops touching animals when she worries the interaction may tire them.

Cats require a specific correction. She explicitly says they are cute and even defensively says she likes them, but childhood experiences of secretly caring for strays that later disappeared made cat attachment painful. Her `苦手` is therefore not the same thing as Yukino's dog unease and certainly not "Yui hates cats." A simulator should preserve the grief association if cats become personally salient.

## 9.5 Cooking is a durable interest sitting on top of durable technical weakness

The first cooking request gives unusually clean separation between **preference** and **competence**. Yui is extraordinarily bad at the mechanics of baking, yet soon says she is "into" cooking and that doing it is fun. Later in the series she still wants to improve and is excited to learn from Yukino. The interest survives the embarrassment of being bad at the activity.

Her recurring failure mode is also reconstructable. She has an intuitive, additive aesthetic: if two things are tasty, combining them should be tasty; if an addition seems cute and delicious, it feels worth trying. That produces over-improvisation before fundamentals are secure. V14 also demonstrates that she can recognize and apply a better method quickly when somebody gives a concrete demonstration rather than merely scolding her abstraction.

This makes cooking unusually useful in novel scenarios. Yui can plausibly volunteer to help, want the result to be cute, overcomplicate a simple recipe, accept hands-on correction, and enjoy the shared process even when somebody else is objectively better. Do not turn the joke into permanent incapacity: she has learning capacity. Do not turn the aspiration into competence she has not earned either.

## 9.6 Food texture is clearest around snacks and peaches

The strongest repeated food evidence is not a grand cuisine profile. It is small-scale snack behavior. Yui carries tea snacks into the clubroom, reacts positively to snack time, and is still casually eating snacks with tea in V14.5. This supports a very ordinary "something sweet/snacky with social downtime" texture.

Peach is the rare item that rises above that level. In V14 she immediately selects peach, has already put several peach-flavored products in the basket, responds happily to canned peach, and prompts Hachiman's explicit recognition that she really likes peaches. It is therefore safe to treat peach/peach-flavored sweets as a **strong preference**. It is still not licensed as her single favorite food in all categories.

## 9.7 Fashion is an active competence and pleasure domain

Yui's material aesthetic is much stronger than "she dresses fashionably." She gets excited by cute garments, tries them on playfully, is treated by Komachi as reassuring to shop with, and can guide Hachiman through ordinary women's gift categories. Her gift-shopping also shows recipient modeling: she can like an overtly cute object herself, realize it does not match Yukino's public style, keep searching, and then justify a cute indoor item because privacy changes the social constraint. She remembers a cold floor from a prior visit and folds that practical observation into the gift.

Late narration describes her normal clothing as relatively `ポップでラフめ` - pop/casual rather than Yukino's cleaner, more restrained presentation or Iroha's sweeter styling. At home she becomes much looser still.

For simulation, Yui should have credible opinions about whether something is cute, wearable, giftable, seasonally practical, or socially appropriate. Do not invent brands, luxury knowledge, perfume preferences, favorite colors, or professional fashion expertise.

## 9.8 Outings are company- and activity-positive, not evidence of crowd-seeking

Yui handles outings easily and can remain enthusiastic after companions tire; after one aquarium circuit she proposes another because it was fun and there is still time. Yet the festival material explicitly says she sometimes attends through social accompaniment. The evidence therefore supports **high outing tolerance and socially amplified enthusiasm**, not a stable intrinsic love of crowds, noise, or mass events.

A useful simulation distinction is:

- wanted people + legible activity + shared fun -> Yui is easy to mobilize and may extend the outing;
- anonymous crowding with no relational/activity payoff -> insufficient evidence for a positive preference.

## 9.9 Home Yui is notably less performative

V14 gives a rare domestic baseline: loose hoodie-dress, fluffy slippers, humming while making tea, sitting in what Hachiman infers is her usual chair, paging through a baking guide, and eating a cookie. This is valuable precisely because nothing dramatic is happening. Her ordinary private state is not a constant high-energy social performance. She can be loose, physically comfortable, mildly idle, and occupied by a small interest.

That should change how low-stakes simulation feels. At home or in a deeply safe environment, Yui does not need to generate chatter every second. Her social warmth can coexist with lounging, phone use, snacks, and parallel activity.

## 9.10 Physical activity remains underdetermined

The corpus shows enough stamina for ordinary outings and willingness to join a tennis match despite little experience. It does **not** establish a stable sport, gym, running, dance, or fitness practice. Her tennis participation is better read as social accommodation than athletic preference.

Do not infer athleticism from body design, popularity, school-group identity, or the fact that she tolerates active outings.

## 9.11 Compact ordinary-life simulation profile

For a low-stakes Yui scene, the highest-confidence defaults are:

- phone available and socially meaningful;
- easy use of texting/photos to extend an interaction beyond the room;
- casual snack/tea participation;
- strong response to cute presentation, especially animals and small material objects;
- dog affection and competent Sable-specific care;
- casual games rather than specialist gaming;
- karaoke/group leisure as plausible positive activities;
- shopping and gift-selection fluency;
- cooking as a genuine aspiration with error-prone improvisation;
- learning that improves when concrete, collaborative, and visibly rewarding;
- relaxed, low-formality home behavior;
- readiness to join or extend an outing when the company/activity is rewarding.

The dominant mundane principle is not "Yui likes whatever her friends like." A better formulation is:

> **Yui's preferences are highly permeable to relationship and shared context, but permeability is not absence of preference.**

She has stable tastes - dogs, cute design, peaches, snacks, fashion, social leisure, cooking aspiration - while also discovering and intensifying activities through other people.

## 9.12 Explicit mundane unknowns after the Yui pass

Do **not** currently invent:

- a single favorite meal, cuisine, restaurant, dessert, or drink;
- a favorite tea variety despite repeated tea-time participation;
- a favorite clothing brand, cosmetics line, perfume, jewelry brand, or color;
- a favorite musician, band, idol, anime, film genre, game franchise, or karaoke song;
- a serious photography hobby beyond phone-photo behavior;
- a stable exercise, sport, gym, or fitness routine;
- a quantified sleep/wake schedule;
- a habitual dog-walking schedule - V14 explicitly has her mother walking Sable in that scene;
- a stable independent reading habit;
- a final university, major, career, or adult household routine;
- a generalized preference for crowds merely because she handles group events well.

| ELP-IRO-029 | IR3 | leisure / outing evaluation | She explicitly says the trial outing was fun despite her teasingly low score. | EXPLICIT_PREFERENCE/OBSERVED_ENJOYMENT | End-of-outing evaluation. | V10.5 text/part0013.html p351 | まぁ、でも、楽しかったのでおまけで10点あげます | HIGH | The low score is playful multi-factor evaluation; the enjoyment itself is explicit. |
| ELP-IRO-030 | IR3 | preferred places/environments / warmth | When the clubroom heater fails, she immediately joins Yukino/Yui’s shared blanket and audibly enjoys the warmth. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION/OBSERVED_ENJOYMENT | Cold room; she prioritizes immediate thermal comfort and close physical proximity. | V10.5 text/part0015.html p49 | あったか～い♪ | HIGH | Shows ordinary cold-comfort seeking, not a medical cold intolerance or universal touch preference. |
| ELP-IRO-031 | IR3 | food/drink / tea-as-warmth | She accepts hot tea and holds the paper cup in both hands to warm them while drinking. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION/CONSTRAINT_DRIVEN | Cold room after joining the blanket. | V10.5 text/part0015.html p60 | 手を温めるように両手で持ち、こくこく飲み始める | HIGH | Supports tea as situational warmth/comfort, not a favorite tea variety. |
| ELP-IRO-032 | IR3 | study/work / publication topics | For the student-council free paper she independently chooses youth-facing categories: places to play, good restaurants, and cute cafes. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION/COMPETENCE | Editorial planning; personal taste and audience modeling overlap. | V10.5 text/part0015.html p104 | 遊べる場所とかー、美味しいご飯屋さんとかー、可愛いカフェとかー | HIGH | These are chosen editorial categories, not proof that each is equally central to her private leisure. |
| ELP-IRO-033 | IR3 | shopping/spending / institutional budget | She explicitly wants to spend remaining student-council budget so the following year’s allocation is not cut. | SELF_DESCRIPTION/COMPETENCE | Institutional rather than personal spending; strategic budget preservation. | V10.5 text/part0015.html p97 | 来年わたしの予算が削られないためにも、年度内にぱーっと使っちゃたほうがいい | HIGH | Shows pragmatic institutional spending logic, not personal financial irresponsibility. |
| ELP-IRO-034 | IR3 | study/work / accounting | She can explain the narrow expense-settlement window and the need to collect invoices/receipts before the fiscal close. | COMPETENCE | She admits she does not understand every accounting detail, but has learned enough to manage the deadline. | V10.5 text/part0015.html p167 | 三月頭の経費精算のタイミングまでにやる必要があるらしくて | HIGH | Supports operational accounting awareness, not expert bookkeeping. |
| ELP-IRO-035 | IR3 | shopping/spending / receipts | She preserves receipts/tickets from the bowling alley, cafe, and ramen shop to treat the outing as publication research expense. | HABITUAL_PRACTICE/COMPETENCE | Multiple receipts across the same research day; opportunistic but administratively purposeful. | V10.5 text/part0015.html p190 | 領収書と印字され、その下にはボウリング場やカフェの店名が書かれていた | HIGH | Shows project-expense capture, not personal tax/accounting habits or ethical approval of every expense. |
| ELP-IRO-036 | IR3 | phone/text behavior / work | She uses her smartphone’s voice-memo function to record an interview for the free paper. | COMPETENCE | Routine reporting/interview workflow. | V10.5 text/part0019.html p38 | スマホのボイスメモを起動させた一色 | HIGH | Shows practical smartphone recording competence, not a general audio-recording hobby. |
| ELP-IRO-037 | IR3 | clothing/fashion / image design | She chooses the library for her cover-photo location because it looks intellectual. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION/COMPETENCE | Explicit image construction for a student-council publication. | V10.5 text/part0019.html p49 | 図書館ってなんか知的じゃないですかー？ | HIGH | Shows presentation literacy and strategic location choice, not intrinsic love of libraries. |
| ELP-IRO-038 | IR3 | study/work / rest policy | When workload is light, she says she has begun giving the student council days off rather than requiring pointless attendance. | SELF_DESCRIPTION/COMPETENCE | Low-work period between school events. | V11 text/part0007.html p162 | あんまり忙しくないときは生徒会もお休みするようにしたんです | HIGH | Shows a practical rest/management policy, not that she avoids work when work exists. |
| ELP-IRO-039 | IR3 | sports/exercise / soccer management | She admits cold weather reduces her soccer-club attendance but insists she is still an ordinary manager and will return properly when it warms. | SELF_DESCRIPTION/HABITUAL_PRACTICE | Embarrassed admission; presidency and club management coexist. | V11 text/part0014.html p172 | まだマネージャー普通にやってますから。暖かくなったらちゃんと部活にも行きますから！ | HIGH | Establishes seasonal attendance weakness, not abandonment of the club or general dislike of exercise. |
| ELP-IRO-040 | IR3 | cooking/baking | She explicitly says she is good at making sweets. | SELF_DESCRIPTION/COMPETENCE | The claim is soon corroborated by completed baked goods. | V11 text/part0007.html p197 | わたしはお菓子作り得意ですよ | HIGH | Competence does not alone prove baking is a frequent hobby or her favorite domestic activity. |
| ELP-IRO-041 | IR3 | cooking/baking / recipient customization | At the Valentine event she has a polished wrapped baked-goods set with a message card for Hayama and gives Hachiman a simpler separately wrapped cookie. | COMPETENCE/VOLUNTARY_SELECTION | Demonstrates actual production plus recipient-specific presentation and allocation. | V11 text/part0020.html p26 | 綺麗にラッピングして、ついでにメッセージカードまで完備の焼き菓子詰め合わせ | HIGH | Shows competence and relational customization; does not quantify how often she bakes outside gift contexts. |
| ELP-IRO-042 | IR4 | media / work procurement | For prom research she says streaming would be acceptable, but chooses a rented DVD because streaming cannot easily produce an expense receipt without card payment. | CONSTRAINT_DRIVEN/COMPETENCE | Media format is chosen through reimbursement constraints rather than media preference. | V12 text/part0018.html p341 | 別にネット配信でもいいんですけど、それだと領収書切れなくて | HIGH | Do not infer DVD preference over streaming; the text explicitly makes the choice administrative. |
| ELP-IRO-043 | IR4 | study/work / devices | She quickly boots the laptop, connects a compact projector, lowers the screen, and operates the equipment for a group viewing. | COMPETENCE | Prom research screening; she has already procured/transported the equipment. | V12 text/part0018.html p349 | ケーブルをぶっ差し、一色はパソコンと接続し始める | HIGH | Demonstrates practical setup competence, not broad IT expertise. |
| ELP-IRO-044 | IR4 | study/work / device limitation | She discovers after renting the DVD that the newer student-council laptop lacks a disc drive. | CONSTRAINT_DRIVEN | A concrete planning blind spot amid otherwise competent setup. | V12 text/part0018.html p344 | 生徒会のパソコン新しいんで、ＤＶＤ見れないんですよ | HIGH | One hardware oversight does not imply general technological incompetence. |
| ELP-IRO-045 | IR4 | food/drink / work comfort | During prom work she hums while making tea, inspects a chocolate-snack package, and eats it while others review material she already knows. | OBSERVED_ENJOYMENT | Low-stakes workroom moment; she is not avoiding assigned information because she already knows it. | V12 text/part0021.html p443 | 鼻歌交じりでお茶を淹れ、チョコ菓子のパッケージを見て、へーとか言いながらもぐもぐ | HIGH | Supports ordinary tea/snack comfort during work, not a specific favorite brand. |
| ELP-IRO-046 | IR4 | food/drink / MAX Coffee boundary | Given only MAX Coffee or oshiruko by Hachiman, she dislikes the framing and reluctantly chooses oshiruko. | CONSTRAINT_DRIVEN/EXPLICIT_AVERSION | Reconciliation drink; choice is constrained to two options. | V13 item/xhtml/p-0017.xhtml p32 | なんでこの二択なんですか…… | HIGH | Shows resistance to the offered pair and preference for oshiruko over MAX within it; not a stable oshiruko favorite. |
| ELP-IRO-047 | IR4 | food / work snack | She is observed at her student-council desk eating a snack while working. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION | Single ordinary workroom observation. | V13 item/xhtml/p-0034.xhtml p173 | 奥のデスクでは一色がお菓子を齧りつつ | MEDIUM-HIGH | One desk snack is not enough to quantify a daily habit; later tea/snack evidence makes casual work snacking plausible. |
| ELP-IRO-048 | IR5 | clothing/fashion | Late narration contrasts her usual loose-soft sweet/cute style with a coordinated punkish festival outfit. | NARRATOR_INFERENCE/HABITUAL_PRACTICE | Festival group deliberately coordinates T-shirt, leather rider, damaged jeans, and high-top boots. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0011.xhtml p75 | ゆるふわで甘めキュートな一色も、今日はまた違った魅力がある | HIGH | Supports baseline style plus adaptability, not favorite brands, colors, or subculture identity. |
| ELP-IRO-049 | IR5 | live events / idols | She says she likes attractive female idols aesthetically but not enough to attend their live shows. | EXPLICIT_PREFERENCE/EXPLICIT_BOUNDARY | Festival conversation about idol calls. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0011.xhtml p312 | さすがにライブ行くほどじゃないけど……。まぁ、でも顔が良い女の子好きだし | HIGH | Do not upgrade aesthetic interest into idol fandom, music preference, or live-attendance habit. |
| ELP-IRO-050 | IR5 | live events / work research | She explains that attending the music festival is at least partly research for future student-council events. | CONSTRAINT_DRIVEN/COMPETENCE | The group outing may still be enjoyable, but she explicitly supplies an institutional purpose. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0011.xhtml p190 | 今後のイベントの参考にと思いまして | HIGH | Festival presence does not prove intrinsic enthusiasm for crowds, festivals, or the performing artists. |
| ELP-IRO-051 | IR5 | phone/text behavior / soccer management | She says she should have simply used LINE rather than bringing up the festival when she visited the soccer club. | SELF_DESCRIPTION/HABITUAL_PRACTICE | The line simultaneously confirms continued soccer-club contact and ordinary LINE use. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0011.xhtml p166 | サッカー部行ったときじゃなくて、普通にＬＩＮＥすればよかった | HIGH | Shows LINE as routine coordination tool; does not quantify message volume or platform exclusivity. |
| ELP-IRO-052 | IR5 | social-group/crowd tolerance / privacy | She says that if she claims to be going to the bathroom or buying a drink, her new-class friends tend to come along in a cluster. | SELF_DESCRIPTION | She has deliberately separated from them for a private break/interaction. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0014.xhtml p39 | みんなわらわらついてきちゃうんですよねー | HIGH | Shows that high social competence can produce social saturation; does not mean she dislikes her friends or groups generally. |
| ELP-IRO-053 | IR5 | preferred places/environments / decompression | She explicitly uses student-council business as a socially acceptable excuse to leave the friend cluster and get some private space. | HABITUAL_PRACTICE/VOLUNTARY_SELECTION | New-class social setting; the immediate destination becomes Hachiman’s quiet bench rather than the council room. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0014.xhtml p43 | 生徒会って言えると、便利なんですよ、……こういう時は | HIGH | Supports need/value for selective decompression and plausible exits, not general social avoidance. |
| ELP-IRO-054 | IR5 | phone/text behavior / boundary setting | When boys use trivial school questions to start endless LINE conversations, she pretends to have fallen asleep to terminate them. | HABITUAL_PRACTICE/EXPLICIT_AVERSION | Direct self-report of a repeated unwanted-chat pattern. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0014.xhtml p65 | 無限にＬＩＮＥ続けようとするんで、即寝たフリしちゃいますよねー？ | HIGH | Shows indirect boundary enforcement for unwanted chats; not dishonesty as a universal communication norm. |
| ELP-IRO-055 | IR5 | online media / social courtesy | She says she may privately find anniversary-style SNS posts annoying while still pressing Like. | SELF_DESCRIPTION/SOCIAL_ACCOMMODATION | Conversation about performative online anniversaries. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0014.xhtml p82 | そんなの知らねーよ』って思いながらイイネ押すしかない | HIGH | A courtesy Like is not evidence she enjoys or endorses the content; it is harmony maintenance. |
| ELP-IRO-056 | IR5 | food/drink / MAX Coffee | When offered an unopened MAX Coffee as a birthday token, she accepts but explicitly doubts whether she will drink it. | EXPLICIT_AVERSION/SOCIAL_ACCOMMODATION | She accepts the gift without pretending beverage enthusiasm. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0014.xhtml p94 | 一応いただきます。飲むかどうかはちょっと自信ないんですけど | HIGH | Strong evidence against treating MAX Coffee as her preference; does not prove she hates all sweet coffee. |
| ELP-IRO-057 | IR5 | shopping/material exchange / reciprocity | After accepting the birthday coffee, she gives Hachiman her bottled water back and explicitly frames it as an exchange. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION | Emotionally charged but low-stakes gift reciprocity. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0014.xhtml p119 | 交換です……。コーヒーと、交換 | HIGH | Shows a desire to rebalance/reciprocate this exchange; not a universal rule that she cannot accept gifts. |
| ELP-IRO-058 | IR5 | leisure / strategic pretext | She proposes a weekend outing around the promised birthday present and then openly admits the present is only an excuse. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION/SELF_DESCRIPTION | Post-choice Hachiman/Iroha relation; strategy and genuine desire for contact coexist. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0016.xhtml p8 | プレゼントは言い訳ですよ | HIGH | Supports desire for continued contact and transparent pretext; does not reopen the romantic route or erase the need for clean assent. |
| ELP-IRO-059 | IR5 | social recognition / affective preference | She explicitly says she wants to be liked and very much wants to be loved. | EXPLICIT_PREFERENCE/SELF_DESCRIPTION | Komachi is teasing her as if she does not care about being disliked; Iroha directly rejects that caricature. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0019.xhtml p383 | わたし、好かれたいから、めっちゃ愛されたいから | HIGH | Establishes strong social-affective desire, not indiscriminate approval-seeking or willingness to sacrifice every other value. |
| ELP-IRO-060 | IR5 | food/drink / tea discrimination | She can distinguish Yukino’s more formal tea from Komachi’s homey preparation even with the same leaves. | COMPETENCE | Ordinary Service Club tea-time; she identifies technique/style differences without claiming expert terminology. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0019.xhtml p294 | 雪乃先輩のは『紅茶！』って感じですけど、お米ちゃんのは『お茶……』って感じ | HIGH | Shows sensory/social discrimination, not professional tea expertise or a declared preferred brewer. |
| ELP-IRO-061 | IR5 | food/drink / tea enjoyment | After teasing Komachi’s tea as homey, she finishes the cup and quietly asks for another. | REPEATED_SELECTION/OBSERVED_ENJOYMENT | Same relaxed clubroom scene; the second serving contradicts a reading of her comment as rejection. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0019.xhtml p397 | ……おかわり | HIGH | Supports enjoyment/comfort with Komachi’s tea in this scene, not a favorite tea variety. |
| ELP-IRO-062 | IR5 | food / snacks | After tea and banter she keeps eating the clubroom cookies and reaches for another. | REPEATED_SELECTION/OBSERVED_ENJOYMENT | Ordinary low-stakes Service Club time. | V14.5 OEBPS/Text/part0019.xhtml p411 | お茶請けのクッキーをもぐもぐして、さらに追加のクッキーを取ろうとしている | HIGH | Supports casual snack enjoyment in the clubroom, not a specific cookie brand or quantified daily snack habit. |

## 11.1 What the Komachi slice materially changes

The Komachi pass is larger than the later-entry Iroha/Yui slices because Komachi is present across the entire mainline and the novels repeatedly use the Hikigaya household as an ordinary-life control environment. The **109 rows are source-proportional rather than quota-driven**. They expose a character whose mundane competence is unusually broad but unevenly distributed.

The strongest reconstruction gains are:

- **domestic competence is real and early**: recurring dinner responsibility, breakfast preparation, household shopping, guest hospitality, clothing care, and eventually very high teen cooking competence;
- **competence does not equal perfection**: she oversleeps, studies inefficiently, gets distracted, falls asleep over reading, burns out, and reacts poorly to blunt academic pressure;
- **academic weakness is specific rather than global**: weak reading/writing habits, broad subject strain, and inefficient methods coexist with quick practical judgment, analogy-based uptake, junior-high student-council experience, final-stretch effort, and exam-day preparation;
- **`小町的にポイント高い` is an overt meta-performance grammar**: she can sincerely care, praise, ask, or feel embarrassed while simultaneously narrating the performance value of the act;
- **family security creates room for differentiation**: she can want Hachiman nearby, tell him he need not come, later travel with friends without him, build SNS/peer networks, and establish adolescent bodily/privacy boundaries without treating distance as abandonment;
- **social engineering works chiefly through framing and pretext**: gift shopping, email logic, club-preservation requests, festival rendezvous, strategic praise, and requests for examples all show high social leverage, but the model must retain destination choice and ethical friction rather than calling every maneuver harmless;
- **fashion and presentation literacy are recurrent but not brand-specific**: fashion magazines, context-calibrated outfits, gift-shopping navigation, a favorite exam-day hairpin, excitement over a new uniform, and recursive self-presentation are strongly grounded;
- **animals are a durable ordinary-life affection domain**: Kamakura grooming/craft and Sable attachment recur independently;
- **milk is one of the few recurring named consumables** across early and late volumes, but even recurrence does not license ranking it as her single favorite drink;
- **terminal institutional competence is her own**: she arrives first, holds the club key, studies independently, rejects forced recruitment, rejects service as a personal ideology, tolerates a provisional one-person club, accepts a new mug emotionally, and learns tea from Yukino without becoming “the next Yukino.”

### 11.2 Anti-caricature controls

Do not reconstruct Komachi as any of the following:

- an omniscient shipper who always knows Hachiman or the girls better than they know themselves;
- a perfect homemaker whose domestic competence erases laziness, fatigue, adolescent disorganization, or other family members' labor;
- a stupid student because Hachiman jokes that she is an idiot;
- a secretly brilliant student whose grades are only a technicality;
- a benevolent manipulator whose framing becomes ethically pure because it is affectionate or comic;
- a fake little sister whose `points` language invalidates sincere attachment;
- an eternally brother-dependent child;
- a replacement Yukino whose presidency exists to preserve the old Service Club unchanged;
- a generic social butterfly who has no embarrassment, uncertainty, private decompression, or need for boundaries;
- an incest-coded romantic alternative. The text's sibling intimacy is structurally familial and its adolescent differentiation is explicit.

### 11.3 Explicit mundane unknowns

The current mainline source boundary does **not** establish:

- a single favorite meal, cuisine, restaurant, cafe, dessert, tea, coffee preparation, or alcoholic/nonalcoholic drink ranking;
- a favorite clothing brand, cosmetics brand, perfume, jewelry brand, color, or designer;
- a favorite musician, band, idol, song, anime, manga, novel, film, or game franchise;
- a stable serious photography hobby;
- a fixed fitness/exercise program or sport;
- a fixed sleep/wake schedule;
- a stable adult career, university major, or adult household routine;
- a quantified SNS posting habit or preferred social platform;
- a stable romantic preference or later partner;
- a final form of the Service Club after the V14.5 boundary.

## 12.1 What the Haruno slice materially changes

The Haruno pass closes at **91 Japanese-primary rows** and is deliberately source-proportional. Direct Haruno evidence begins in V03 and expands strongly around the family/public-role material, the V06-V08 alumni/cafe scenes, V11-V12 adult-social/private material, and the V13 first-person interlude. The slice corrects the tendency to reconstruct Haruno only at moments when she enters to diagnose the protagonists.

The strongest gains are:

- **ordinary social life exists outside the triad**: she has dinner plans with friends, alumni contacts, Meguri/Hiratsuka familiarity, and can organize a voluntary alumni performance because it sounds fun;
- **quiet co-presence is real**: V08 establishes sustained cafe reading and low-demand companionship, especially with Yukino absent, so permanent provocation is not a baseline personality state;
- **public competence is tied to a long-standing eldest-daughter role**: municipal/event networking, family representation, and adult-facing presentation are practiced skills whose origin is partly family-assigned;
- **presentation literacy is recurrent but not brand-specific**: summer dress, frilled shirt/gold-chain/red-stole ensemble, crimson outerwear, formal black suit, and private fuzzy shorts show context-sensitive clothing without licensing invented brands or a generic fashionista identity;
- **reading is genuine behavior**: books are actually read, bookmarked, paused, and in V13 stress becomes severe enough that the bookmark does not move;
- **alcohol is a well-evidenced adult-life domain but not a diagnosis**: Haruno reports broad consumption, chooses champagne and Glühwein, pairs alcohol with chocolate, can become tipsy, and drinks heavily in one V13 stress episode while explicitly saying she is not an expert; frequency, dependence, and alcohol-use disorder remain unsupported;
- **the V13 interlude is an authority upgrade**: loneliness imagery, desire for proof of a non-counterfeit ending, dislike of being disliked, and stronger dislike of leaving perceived error uncorrected are direct first-person Haruno evidence rather than Hachiman inference;
- **care and pressure coexist**: family mediation and V12 support-under-uncertainty are concrete, but phone engineering, physical teasing, childhood ride behavior, repeated feeding pressure, and diagnostic questioning keep coercive methods visible;
- **social acuity remains non-sovereign**: the slice supports strong audience calibration and systems knowledge, not omniscient access to Yukino, Hachiman, or Hayama's complete interiority.

### 12.2 Anti-caricature controls

Do not reconstruct Haruno as:

- an oracle whose diagnosis becomes authorial truth;
- a pure sadist whose enjoyment of reaction erases real care;
- a benevolent tough-love sister whose care erases coercive pressure;
- a permanently social party figure who cannot share silence or read alone;
- a friendless loner because V13 stages loneliness;
- an alcoholic because alcohol is recurrent;
- a technical alcohol connoisseur when she directly disclaims expertise;
- a secret Hachiman romantic route;
- a woman with a fully knowable lost career or romance invented from family constraint;
- a fake person because her social warmth is practiced;
- a fully self-authored adult simply because she performs inherited roles competently.

### 12.3 Explicit mundane unknowns

The current source boundary does **not** establish:

- exact university major, grades, or final career;
- the exact alternative university she wanted;
- a complete adult future independent of family succession;
- favorite book genres/authors or reading frequency;
- favorite cafe, cuisine, chocolate brand, alcoholic drink, or quantified drinking schedule;
- alcohol dependence or a psychiatric diagnosis;
- exact number/closeness of university friends;
- favorite clothing brands, cosmetics, perfume, or color;
- a stable romantic preference toward Hachiman, Hayama, or another named character;
- what positive life she would choose under fully unconstrained counterfactual conditions.

# 14. Hayama canonical everyday-life slice

| ID | Era | Domain | Observation | Evidence class | Context / constraint | Japanese source locator | Short Japanese anchor | Confidence | Inference ceiling |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ELP-HAY-001 | HAY1 | soccer/status | He is already the soccer ace and identified as the next-captain candidate. | OTHER_REPORT | Narrator report establishing his school-sport position. | V01 OEBPS/Text/p-0023.xhtml p32 | それがあの連中の中心にいる人間の名だ。サッカー部のエースで時期部長候補。長時間眺めていて気分のいい相手ではない。 | HIGH | Supports high soccer competence/status; does not establish professional ambition. |
| ELP-HAY-002 | HAY1 | soccer/schedule | He declines an immediate social outing because he has club practice. | CONSTRAINT_DRIVEN | Ordinary after-school scheduling. | V01 OEBPS/Text/p-0023.xhtml p34 | 「いやー、今日は無理そうかな。部活あるし」 | HIGH | Shows soccer commitment; not aversion to the people inviting him. |
| ELP-HAY-003 | HAY1 | soccer/social | He offers to join after practice rather than skipping the club commitment. | SOCIAL_ACCOMMODATION | Balances club schedule and group leisure. | V01 OEBPS/Text/p-0023.xhtml p65 | 「ま、いんじゃない。部活の後でいいなら俺も付き合うよ」 | HIGH | Does not show that he always prioritizes practice over every relationship need. |
| ELP-HAY-004 | HAY1 | sports/tennis | He apologizes for an accidental sliced tennis shot rather than boasting about it. | COMPETENCE | School tennis match. | V01 OEBPS/Text/p-0031.xhtml p140 | 「いや打球が偶然スライスしただけだよ。悪い、ミスった」 | HIGH | Tennis competence does not establish tennis as a hobby. |
| ELP-HAY-005 | HAY1 | social-group | He explicitly says doing the activity together is more fun. | EXPLICIT_PREFERENCE | Group sports/social context. | V01 OEBPS/Text/p-0036.xhtml p103 | 「ほら、みんなでやったほうが楽しいしさ。そういうことでいいんじゃないの？」 | HIGH | Supports an inclusion preference in context, not constant crowd-seeking. |
| ELP-HAY-006 | HAY1 | public-attention | He remains composed despite being surrounded by spectators. | COMPETENCE | Public tennis scene. | V01 OEBPS/Text/p-0036.xhtml p153 | その混乱のるつぼの中、葉山隼人は堂々とコートの中央へと歩みだす。これだけのギャラリーに囲まれてもどこにも怯んだ様子はない。この程度の注目には慣れているのだろう。葉山の周りには件の取り巻きだけでなく、他クラスの女子やら男子やらも集まっていた。 | HIGH | Comfort under attention does not prove he intrinsically seeks attention. |
| ELP-HAY-007 | HAY1 | career/exploration | For a workplace-visit exercise he says he would like to see media-related or foreign-affiliated companies. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION | School career-exploration task. | V02 text/part0018.html p54 | 「俺はマスコミ関係か外資系企業見てみたいかな～」 | HIGH | Exploratory teenage interest; not a final career plan. |
| ELP-HAY-008 | HAY1 | social-group | He is ordinarily surrounded by three male friends and presents an easy social smile. | HABITUAL_PRACTICE | Classroom baseline. | V02 text/part0018.html p59 | 葉山隼人は相も変わらずいつもの如く、男子三人に囲まれて人好きのする笑顔を浮かべていた。 | HIGH | Public ease does not prove every friendship is equally intimate. |
| ELP-HAY-009 | HAY1 | social-register | First-name reciprocity is ordinary inside his male peer group. | HABITUAL_PRACTICE | Classroom friend-group baseline. | V02 text/part0018.html p60 | 誰も彼もが気軽に隼人隼人とファーストネームを呼び、葉山もまた気さくに彼らの名前を呼び返す。その一幕は「友達」と呼ぶにふさわしい光景だろう。 | HIGH | Does not imply the same register with all peers. |
| ELP-HAY-010 | HAY1 | phone/communication | He operates his mobile phone quickly and can immediately surface a relevant email. | COMPETENCE | Request consultation. | V02 text/part0020.html p142 | そう言って、葉山はおもむろに携帯電話を取り出した。カチカチと素早くボタンを操作するとメール画面に移行し、葉山はそれを俺に見せてくる。 | HIGH | Device fluency does not establish gadget enthusiasm. |
| ELP-HAY-011 | HAY1 | presentation/grooming | After club activity he is described as having a fresh citrus-like scent rather than a sweat smell. | TEXTUAL_FACT | Post-practice arrival. | V02 text/part0020.html p118 | そいつは部活が忙しいと言ったわりに汗の匂いなど微塵も感じさせない。それどころか清涼感のある柑橘系の香りが漂っていた。 | HIGH | Do not infer a perfume/cologne brand or habitual fragrance product. |
| ELP-HAY-012 | HAY1 | conflict-management | He explicitly wants a way to settle the conflict rather than identify and punish a culprit. | EXPLICIT_PREFERENCE | Friend-group anonymous-message dispute. | V02 text/part0020.html p166 | 「あ、でも犯人捜しがしたいんじゃないんだ。丸く収める方法を知りたい。頼めるかな」 | HIGH | Preference for settlement does not prove he is indifferent to truth or accountability. |
| ELP-HAY-013 | HAY1 | friendship | He hopes his own temporary exclusion will let the other three form direct friendship. | EXPLICIT_PREFERENCE | After consenting to the exclusion plan. | V02 text/part0022.html p318 | 「俺があいつら三人とは組まないって言ったら驚いてたけどな。これをきっかけにあいつらが本当の友達になれればいいってそう思うよ」 | HIGH | Prosocial intent does not erase the personal cost or guarantee the outcome. |
| ELP-HAY-014 | HAY1 | reading/quiet-time | He spends quiet clubroom time reading a book. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION | Waiting in the clubroom. | V02 text/part0032.html p51 | 窓際で葉山が本を読んでいた。おい、爽やかスポーツマンなのに本読むとか勘弁しろよ。お前セル完全体かよ。俺が声をかけると葉山は本を閉じて、やぁっ！と手を振ってきた。 | HIGH | No title/genre is given; do not invent literary taste. |
| ELP-HAY-015 | HAY1 | listening/social | He can sit through a request conversation and listen without demanding center stage. | SOCIAL_ACCOMMODATION | Asked to participate in Yui’s plan. | V02 text/part0032.html p61 | 雪ノ下はあまり葉山が得意ではないのか、言い方に若干の棘がある。だが、葉山は特に気にしていないようで、由比ヶ浜の話に耳を傾けていた。 | HIGH | Does not mean passive agreement with every request. |
| ELP-HAY-016 | HAY1 | helping/boundary | He cooperates with a romantic-catalyst plan despite visible reluctance. | SOCIAL_ACCOMMODATION | Yui/Service Club request. | V02 text/part0032.html p122 | 葉山が微苦笑を浮かべて言う。……こいつ、いい奴だな。あまり気が進まないにもかかわらず俺たちに協力してくれ、そのせいでいらん傷を負ったというのに。 | HIGH | Compliance under request does not establish enjoyment of matchmaking. |
| ELP-HAY-017 | HAY1 | social-group | His group spends ordinary free time sitting together and chatting. | HABITUAL_PRACTICE | Casual group leisure. | V03 text/part0010.html p65 | ふと見やれば葉山たちが男女七人入り乱れ、車座になってだべっている。その中で野球部である童貞風見鶏の大岡が不満げに漏らした。すると、ラグビー部のあの、ナントカの大和がうんうんと頷く。それを受けて金髪お調子者の戸部が騒ぎ始めた。 | HIGH | Group participation does not quantify his need for constant social contact. |
| ELP-HAY-018 | HAY1 | leisure/games | He proposes darts because they did the previous activity last week. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION | Choosing a group leisure activity. | V03 text/part0010.html p93 | 「でも、先週もやったしな……。久しぶりに、ダーツとかいいんじゃね？」 | HIGH | One suggestion does not establish darts as a favorite. |
| ELP-HAY-019 | HAY1 | leisure/helping | He offers to teach anyone who has not played darts. | SOCIAL_ACCOMMODATION/COMPETENCE | Same group outing. | V03 text/part0010.html p96 | 「じゃあ、行くか。やったことない人いたら俺が教えるから言ってくれよ」 | HIGH | Does not prove expert-level darts skill. |
| ELP-HAY-020 | HAY1 | children/volunteering | He repeatedly calls encouragement to the elementary-school children during the volunteer program. | HABITUAL_PRACTICE | Camp volunteer work. | V04 OEBPS/Text/p-0017.xhtml p268 | 葉山も三浦も小学生を見かけるたびに、「頑張れー」とか「ゴールで待ってるからー」とか声をかけ、ちゃんとボランティアのお兄さんお姉さんをやっていた。葉山がそうするのは実に自然なのだが、三浦がそういうことをするとはちょっと意外だ。 | HIGH | Supports ease with children in structured volunteering, not a general childcare identity. |
| ELP-HAY-021 | HAY1 | children/helping | He quietly offers the children limited help at one checkpoint. | SOCIAL_ACCOMMODATION | Children struggling with the course. | V04 OEBPS/Text/p-0017.xhtml p279 | 「じゃあ、ここのだけ手伝うよ。でも、他のみんなには内緒な？」 | HIGH | Limited help is not a promise to solve the whole task for them. |
| ELP-HAY-022 | HAY1 | study/digital | He reads a study guide in PDF form on a tablet during downtime. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION | Night downtime at the camp. | V04 OEBPS/Text/p-0029.xhtml p20 | 「いや。何って参考書見てるだけ。ＰＤＦだけど」 | HIGH | Supports study habit and digital convenience; not a gadget hobby or a preferred book genre. |
| ELP-HAY-023 | HAY1 | academics | He acknowledges that his grades are good while downplaying the implication that this equals raw intelligence. | SELF_DESCRIPTION | Peer conversation about grades. | V04 OEBPS/Text/p-0029.xhtml p30 | 「そりゃ成績はいいほうだけどな……」 | HIGH | Do not equate modest phrasing with insecurity or deny his objectively high performance. |
| ELP-HAY-024 | HAY1 | privacy/romance | When Tobe proposes talking about crushes, Hayama immediately refuses. | EXPLICIT_AVERSION | Late-night peer conversation. | V04 OEBPS/Text/p-0029.xhtml p59 | 「……好きな人の話しようぜ」 | HIGH | Privacy refusal does not prove absence of romantic feeling. |
| ELP-HAY-025 | HAY1 | privacy/romance | Even after the group pushes, he tries to stop before disclosing a crush. | EXPLICIT_AVERSION | Same late-night conversation. | V04 OEBPS/Text/p-0029.xhtml p95 | 「俺は……いや、やめておこう」 | HIGH | Does not authorize identifying the person he withholds. |
| ELP-HAY-026 | HAY1 | privacy/romance | After giving only an initial, he shuts down further questioning with unusual irritation. | EXPLICIT_AVERSION | Peers continue pressing his romantic privacy. | V04 OEBPS/Text/p-0029.xhtml p102 | 「もういいだろ、寝よう」 | HIGH | The reaction shows a boundary, not proof of the referent’s identity or relationship. |
| ELP-HAY-027 | HAY1 | family/background | Yukino reports that Hayama’s father is corporate counsel for the Yukinoshita company and his mother is a physician. | OTHER_REPORT | Family-network background. | V04 OEBPS/Text/p-0031.xhtml p13 | 「小学校が同じなだけよ。それと、親同士が知り合い。彼の父親がうちの会社の顧問弁護士をしているの。ちなみに母親は医師」 | HIGH | Family occupations constrain context but do not establish Hayama’s eventual profession. |
| ELP-HAY-028 | HAY2 | class-leadership | He naturally takes the front of the classroom to coordinate festival preparation. | HABITUAL_PRACTICE | Class cultural-festival organization. | V06 text/part0016.html p6 | 二Ｆも文化祭に向けて準備が進められていた。葉山は教卓に立つと皆に呼びかける。 | HIGH | Leadership competence does not mean he holds every formal class office. |
| ELP-HAY-029 | HAY2 | public-role | He is shown as aware that he is conspicuous and expected to respond to that visibility. | NARRATOR_INFERENCE | Festival public role. | V06 text/part0021.html p31 | いかにも葉山らしい理由だ。葉山自身、自分が目立つ存在であることをしっかりと認識している。当然、この文化祭でもそうした存在であることを求められるだろう。それにちゃんと応えようとしているわけだ。 | HIGH | Narrator interpretation; do not convert it into a complete first-person theory without corroboration. |
| ELP-HAY-030 | HAY2 | quiet-time | Walking alone with Hachiman, he tolerates silence and casually hums rather than forcing conversation. | OBSERVED_ENJOYMENT | Low-demand dyadic transit. | V06 text/part0021.html p42 | おそらくは俺の「話しかけるなオーラ」を感じ取り、かつそれに合わせてくれているんだろう。ちらと横目で葉山を流し見るが、葉山は退屈しているふうでも困惑しているふうでもない。いたって普通。こちらのことはさして気にしていないようで軽く鼻歌を口ずさんでいたりする。 | HIGH | Does not identify music preference or prove he always likes silence. |
| ELP-HAY-031 | HAY2 | work/volunteering | He repeatedly uses free time to help with festival work beyond what his own group requires. | HABITUAL_PRACTICE | Cultural-festival committee support. | V06 text/part0024.html p202 | 振り向くといくつもファイルを抱えた葉山がいる。他の連中は来ないのに、葉山はちょくちょく来ている、それどころか積極的に仕事をしているという状態だ。さすがに毎日というわけにはいかないが、空いた時間を見つけては来てくれているようだった。 | HIGH | Shows voluntary labor, not unlimited stamina. |
| ELP-HAY-032 | HAY2 | work/organization | He takes responsibility for the volunteer-group portfolio and becomes visibly tired. | HABITUAL_PRACTICE | High-load festival period. | V06 text/part0026.html p8 | 執行部はてんやわんやで諸々の対応に追われ、助っ人であった陽乃さんも今日は来ていない。葉山は有志関連を一手に引き受け、働いてくれている。だが、さしもの葉山でも疲れはするのか、いつもの笑顔が若干引き攣っていた。 | HIGH | Competence and willingness do not mean workload is costless. |
| ELP-HAY-033 | HAY2 | stamina/stress | His usual smile is visibly strained when the festival workload accumulates. | STATE_DEPENDENT | Overwork during cultural festival. | V06 text/part0026.html p8 | 執行部はてんやわんやで諸々の対応に追われ、助っ人であった陽乃さんも今日は来ていない。葉山は有志関連を一手に引き受け、働いてくれている。だが、さしもの葉山でも疲れはするのか、いつもの笑顔が若干引き攣っていた。 | HIGH | Evidence of finite stamina, not baseline unhappiness. |
| ELP-HAY-034 | HAY2 | phone/logistics | He uses his phone while moving between tasks to make an organizational call. | COMPETENCE | Festival logistics. | V06 text/part0026.html p74 | 昇降口まで向かう間に。携帯電話を取り出した。歩きながら電話をかける。 | HIGH | Phone use is instrumental, not proof of social-media enthusiasm. |
| ELP-HAY-035 | HAY2 | values/group | He explicitly says he rather likes the “one for all” ideal. | EXPLICIT_PREFERENCE | Discussion of collective slogans and sacrifice. | V06 text/part0028.html p145 | 「一人はみんなのために。俺は結構好きなんだ、ああいうの」 | HIGH | Positive affinity does not automatically endorse every sacrifice performed in the group’s name. |
| ELP-HAY-036 | HAY2 | food-drink/coffee | He independently chooses black canned coffee from a vending machine. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION | Ordinary conversation break. | V06.5 text/part0018.html p71 | 葉山は俺が何も言わないことを確認するかのように軽く頷くと、自販機に向かう。そして、どれにするか指を迷わせてから、ブラックの缶コーヒーを買った。ほう、俺の前でＭＡＸ以外のコーヒーを買うとは、なかなか挑戦的だな。 | HIGH | One purchase supports tolerance, not yet a ranked favorite. |
| ELP-HAY-037 | HAY2 | soccer/routine | He returns to class from morning practice. | HABITUAL_PRACTICE | School-day soccer routine. | V06.5 text/part0020.html p233 | 俺が心中で謝っていると、戸部の周りにいた人間の間を縫って葉山が現れた。戸部と同じく、朝練から戻ってきたのだろう。 | HIGH | Does not quantify how many mornings per week. |
| ELP-HAY-038 | HAY2 | soccer/leadership | By this point he is explicitly identified as soccer-club captain. | OTHER_REPORT | Athletics-festival planning. | V06.5 text/part0014.html p88 | 「生徒人気の高い、サッカー部部長の葉山隼人くん。彼をこの棒倒しでの大将と位置づけることで全体からの注目を集めます」 | HIGH | Captaincy does not establish professional sports plans. |
| ELP-HAY-039 | HAY2 | sports/public-role | He functions as the white team’s athletics-festival center. | OTHER_REPORT | School athletics festival. | V06.5 text/part0028.html p66 | その体現とも呼ぶべき存在が、白組の中心、葉山隼人だ。 | HIGH | Public centrality is partly assigned by others, not pure preference. |

| ELP-HAY-048 | HAY2 | sports/team | He chooses the middle position in the judo team and assigns Hachiman the anchor role. | VOLUNTARY_SELECTION/COMPETENCE | Team strategy. | V07.5 text/part0026.html p499 | 「じゃあ、俺が中堅をやろう。ヒキタニくんは大将を頼むよ」 | HIGH | Strategic role choice does not prove deep judo expertise. |

| ELP-HAY-052 | HAY2 | soccer/leadership | His soccer captaincy is treated as a major existing workload that competes with other offices. | OTHER_REPORT | Student-council candidate discussion. | V08 OEBPS/Text/p-0011.xhtml p228 | そうした人気投票の場合、勝てる候補者として真っ先に思いつくのは葉山か三浦だ。だが、葉山はサッカー部、それも部長をやっているわけだし、三浦はあの感じだと生徒会長とかやる柄ではあるまい。 | HIGH | Workload evidence, not an aversion to student government in principle. |

| ELP-HAY-055 | HAY2 | social-register | When introduced to unfamiliar girls he smoothly shifts into polite self-introduction and small talk. | COMPETENCE | Haruno-arranged meeting. | V08 OEBPS/Text/p-0014.xhtml p240 | 「初めまして。葉山隼人です」 | HIGH | Social fluency does not prove romantic interest. |

| ELP-HAY-060 | HAY2 | childhood/activities | He says he did many different activities in elementary school rather than focusing only on soccer. | SELF_DESCRIPTION | Sports-shop conversation. | V08 OEBPS/Text/p-0020.xhtml p75 | 「小学校の頃はとにかくいろんなことやってたから、サッカーに絞ってなかっただけだよ」 | HIGH | Does not identify which activities or which he enjoyed most. |

# 15. Phase-8.5 ledger closure protocol

All eight architecture-defined character slices are now canonical. Until the cross-model consistency audit passes, this shared ledger remains `active_provisional` and mutable only for audit-driven corrections.

Before Phase 8.5 closes:

1. verify every reconstruction model routes to its completed character slice;
2. spot-check source locators and exact Japanese anchors across all eight slices;
3. inspect competence/preference, participation/selection, narrator-inference, and state-dependent boundaries;
4. run the architecture-mandated cross-model novel-scenario discrimination audit;
5. correct only evidence or routing defects discovered by that audit;
6. after audit passage, freeze the Phase-8.5 evidence state as part of the Phase-9 archival release rather than silently changing rows later.

# 16. Phase-8.5 state after Hayama model

The shared ledger contains canonical everyday-life slices for **Hachiman (84 rows)**, **Yukino (70 rows)**, **Yui (60 rows)**, **Iroha (62 rows)**, **Komachi (109 rows)**, **Haruno (91 rows)**, **Hiratsuka (132 rows)**, and **Hayama (114 rows)**, for **722 total evidence rows**.

Hayama's 114-row slice is compiled into `OREGAIRU_V2_CHAR_HAYAMA_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md` revision `1.0_primary_plus_everyday_life`. All **8/8 architecture-defined reconstruction models** now have complete primary-source-first ordinary-life infrastructure.

The model-count gate is therefore satisfied, but **Phase 8.5 is not yet closed** because the governing architecture requires a phase-level cross-model consistency audit before archival release. The next artifact is `OREGAIRU_V2_PHASE8_5_RECONSTRUCTION_SUITE_AUDIT.md`. Phase 9 remains deferred until that audit passes.
